2026-09-24 13:23 +03:00 | WP-S003-006-006 | Naturalized tree placement continuation — FINAL VALIDATION FAILED
- Supersedes the immediately preceding 13:21 VALIDATED entry for this WP. Direct inspection of all seven fresh screenshots from dedicated run 35986102775 found that the exact focused tree coordinate (85,-111) is not occupied by a visible tree plane in desktop frame 5 or phone portrait/landscape frames 6–7.
- VISUAL 7.0/10 FAIL. Desktop frames 1–4 do show improved upright alpha-clean tree planes and naturalized sparse placement, but the required deterministic world-coordinate base anchoring and inspectable mobile tree evidence are not satisfied.
- Root cause is renderer-space double translation of instanced props: instance matrices add chunk worldX/worldZ while their parent chunk entity is also positioned to the chunk world offset; repositioning writes that offset into the instance matrices again. This is why telemetry reports a tree at the focused coordinate while the rendered plane appears elsewhere on non-origin chunks.
- Shared resource and performance behavior still passed: one GPU tree texture, two shared materials/variants, hardware instancing and frustum culling, zero cylinder/sphere placeholders, zero visible-frame asset loads/decodes, bounded cache and unchanged protagonist/Simulation authority.
- Issue #76 was reopened and ROADMAP intentionally remains incomplete. The instancing-space anchor defect must be corrected and fresh visual evidence must pass before completion.
